# The guns: the 16 weapon types whose shots are the particles of a beam system (vcBeamParticleSystem), fired
# in bursts, with one state machine (0x101DD0). After its WeaponSpec each keeps a second record, of 19
# words, cleared and filled the same way; seven of its words point at the constants its WeaponSpec points at.
# Not read: +0x0C (the constant of the WeaponSpec's second word), +0x28 (0.2 to 0.4: handed over with each
# shot, kept in the particle) and +0x30 (250 or 500). Here the 14 with a name.
struct  GunSpec  4C                                 # a gun type's own record of where its constants are, after its WeaponSpec
field   GunSpec  000  aim_angle           u32        # points at the degrees either side of where it points within which it turns a shot towards its target
field   GunSpec  004  damage              u32        # points at the damage a hit does (WeaponSpec's)
field   GunSpec  008  cost                u32        # points at what a shot takes from its energy (WeaponSpec's)
field   GunSpec  010  range               u32        # points at how far a shot reaches (WeaponSpec's)
field   GunSpec  014  reload              u32        # points at the milliseconds from a shot to the next (WeaponSpec's)
field   GunSpec  018  shots               u32        # points at how many of its shots can be in the air at once: the beam system's particles
field   GunSpec  01C  life                u32        # points at the milliseconds a shot lasts: its range over its speed in 13 of the 16, near it in the rest
field   GunSpec  020  speed               u32        # points at its shots' speed (WeaponSpec's)
field   GunSpec  024  length              u32        # points at how long a shot's streak grows
field   GunSpec  02C  color               u32        # points at its shots' colour: three floats, red, green and blue, 0 to 255
field   GunSpec  034  shot_gap            u32        # points at the milliseconds between the shots of a burst
field   GunSpec  038  shot_gap_random     u32        # points at how many milliseconds more or less, at random
field   GunSpec  03C  burst_gap           u32        # points at the milliseconds from a burst's last shot to the next burst
field   GunSpec  040  burst_gap_random    u32        # points at how many milliseconds more or less, at random
field   GunSpec  044  burst_shots         u32        # points at the shots of a burst
field   GunSpec  048  burst_shots_random  u32        # points at how many shots more or less, at random
0021EDD4  var   PodOfficerArmCannonWeaponGunSpec                 GunSpec

field   GunConstants  0C8  burst_gap           s32
field   GunConstants  0CC  burst_gap_random    s32
field   GunConstants  0D0  burst_shots         s32
field   GunConstants  0D4  burst_shots_random  s32

# What a missile is made to, besides its EntitySpec: a record of 19 words that each of the 8 missile types
# clears and fills in its X_Register just before its EntitySpec, at the start of what the type's objects
# share; a missile keeps its address (its +0x26C and +0x274) and its first word points 0x4C bytes on, at the
# EntitySpec. Each word points at one of the type's constants. Read with Ghidra in the missiles' shared code
# (0x59620 to 0x5B850). Not read: +0x0C (150 to 400), +0x2C, +0x24 and +0x28 (kept in the missile when it
# is set up, the same values as swing and swing_time) and +0x44 (0.25: what its steering is scaled by, with
# a push sideways, while its target is behind it). Here the 4 with a name.
struct  MissileSpec  4C                             # a missile type's record of where its constants are
field   MissileSpec  000  speed                u32   # points at its top speed: what it steers to reach towards its target, and the most it flies at
field   MissileSpec  004  mass                 u32   # points at its mass: the force that steers it over this is what its speed changes by
field   MissileSpec  008  thrust               u32   # points at the most force that steers it; with no target, the force that pushes it straight on
field   MissileSpec  010  damage               u32   # points at the damage it does to what it hits, and at most to what is near
field   MissileSpec  014  life                 u32   # points at the milliseconds it flies before it ends by itself
field   MissileSpec  018  steer_interval       u32   # points at the milliseconds between two times it works out where to steer
field   MissileSpec  01C  launch_speed         u32   # points at the part of its top speed it leaves its launcher with
field   MissileSpec  020  launch_speed_random  u32   # points at how much more or less than that, at random
field   MissileSpec  030  swing                u32   # points at how far off its path each of its warheads swings: half to all of it, at random
field   MissileSpec  034  swing_time           u32   # points at the milliseconds a whole swing, to one side and to the other, is worked out from: three quarters to one and a half of it, at random
field   MissileSpec  038  turn_time            u32   # points at the milliseconds a warhead takes to go once round the path, either way
field   MissileSpec  03C  blast_inner          u32   # points at how near to where it hits an entity takes all its damage
field   MissileSpec  040  blast_outer          u32   # points at how far from where it hits its damage falls to nothing
field   MissileSpec  048  alert_radius         u32   # points at how near to where it hits the AIs of the other side are told of the hit
001EDC78  var   PodOfficerSledMissileMissileSpec                 MissileSpec
001EDEA8  var   FPAMissileMissileSpec                            MissileSpec

# same in the 8 types. A field is the constant MissileSpec's field of that name points at.
struct  MissileConstants  4C                        # a missile type's constants: what its MissileSpec's words point at
field   MissileConstants  000  thrust               f32
field   MissileConstants  008  mass                 f32
field   MissileConstants  00C  speed                f32
field   MissileConstants  010  damage               f32
field   MissileConstants  014  life                 s32
field   MissileConstants  018  steer_interval       s32
field   MissileConstants  01C  launch_speed         f32
field   MissileConstants  020  launch_speed_random  f32
field   MissileConstants  030  swing                f32
field   MissileConstants  034  swing_time           f32
field   MissileConstants  038  turn_time            f32
field   MissileConstants  03C  blast_inner          f32
field   MissileConstants  040  blast_outer          f32
field   MissileConstants  048  alert_radius         f32

# The heavy cannons: the five weapon types (Weapon 39, 40, 42, 43, 44; the railgun is one) whose shot hits
# at once along a ray, a while after it is fired, and leaves a trail (a vcGeometryTrail), with one state
# machine (0x100A10). After its WeaponSpec each keeps a record of 11 words; five of them point at the
# constants its WeaponSpec points at. Not read: +0x0C (the constant of the WeaponSpec's second word). Here
# the 4 with a name.
struct  HeavyCannonSpec  2C                         # a heavy cannon type's own record of where its constants are, after its WeaponSpec

field   HeavyCannonSpec  004  damage        u32      # points at the damage a hit does (WeaponSpec's)
field   HeavyCannonSpec  008  cost          u32      # points at what a shot takes from its energy (WeaponSpec's)
field   HeavyCannonSpec  010  range         u32      # points at how far a shot reaches (WeaponSpec's)
field   HeavyCannonSpec  014  reload        u32      # points at the milliseconds from a shot to the next (WeaponSpec's)
field   HeavyCannonSpec  018  trail_life    u32      # points at the milliseconds each point of its shot's trail lasts
field   HeavyCannonSpec  01C  trail_fade    u32      # points at the last milliseconds of those, over which the point fades
field   HeavyCannonSpec  020  shot_delay    u32      # points at the milliseconds from when it is fired to the shot: the ray is tested and the trail shown then
field   HeavyCannonSpec  024  trail_width   u32      # points at how far each edge of the trail is from its middle
field   HeavyCannonSpec  028  color         u32      # points at the trail's colour: three floats, red, green and blue, 0 to 255

# The chunks: the 16 effect types that throw pieces of a scene (the damage chunks of the characters and of
# the Cyclops, the three sizes of Chunk1), with one state machine (ChunksEffect_StateMachine). What a type's
# objects share starts with how many pieces an effect of it has and its scene (a piece is a copy of one of
# the scene's objects, picked at random), then a record of 13 words its X_Register clears and fills, each
# pointing at one of the type's constants in .rdata; an effect keeps the record's address at its +0xD64.
# Read with Ghidra in the chunks' code (0x10ABE0 to 0x10B680; PENDING.md has how each was told). Not read:
# +0x2C (1.6, or 3 for Chunk1's).
struct  ChunksSpec  34                              # a chunks effect type's record of where its constants are
field   ChunksSpec  000  delay         u32           # points at the milliseconds before it throws its first pieces
field   ChunksSpec  004  throw_time    u32           # points at the milliseconds from when it was set up after which it throws no more
field   ChunksSpec  008  throw_gap     u32           # points at the milliseconds from a throw to the next
field   ChunksSpec  00C  end_wait      u32           # points at the milliseconds from its last throw to its end
field   ChunksSpec  010  throw_count   u32           # points at how many pieces it throws at a time
field   ChunksSpec  014  life          u32           # points at the milliseconds a piece lasts
field   ChunksSpec  018  spin          u32           # points at how fast a piece turns about each of its three axes: three floats
field   ChunksSpec  01C  spin_random   u32           # points at how much more or less than that, at random: three floats
field   ChunksSpec  020  gravity       u32           # points at what pulls a piece where there is gravity, three floats: its place gains this times the square of its time
field   ChunksSpec  024  speed         u32           # points at the speed a piece is thrown with
field   ChunksSpec  028  speed_random  u32           # points at how much more or less than that, at random
field   ChunksSpec  030  throw_angle   u32           # points at the degrees off the effect's direction, at most, a piece is thrown
00224864  var   CyclopsDamageChunkEffectChunksSpec               ChunksSpec
002248E4  var   CharacterDamageChunksLargeWEffectChunksSpec      ChunksSpec
00224964  var   CharacterDamageChunksMedWEffectChunksSpec        ChunksSpec

# The veritechs: a veritech model (VF-1SX, VF-1JX, VF-1BX, VF-1AX, VF-1DX) has ten EntitySpec in what its

# on a level with a terrain, the battloid on one without, the guardian and the fighter, with no pack and with

# mode and pack). One function a model fills the ten (sub_0005C300 the VF-1SX's), and each record's

# The engine's storage devices: the hard disk and the memory units, a record each in a table the engine
# allocates (0x3DC40 bytes a record: its drive's letter first; the first record is the hard disk's, U),
# and what happened to one last. Read by an agent from Ghidra's C and checked here in sub_0012DC90,
# sub_0012DE80 and sub_0012E0B0 (XGetDeviceChanges: a memory unit is a bit by its port, the low 16 for
# the top slot, the high 16 for the bottom one). Not named: 0x248D54, the device the event is of (a
# memory unit's number plus one in sub_0012E0B0, not in the others).
00248D44  var   vcMemoryUnitCount  u32                  # the memory units the engine looks at; nothing writes it: 0
00248D48  var   vcStorageDeviceCount  u32               # the records of vcStorageDevices: vcMemoryUnitCount and one, the hard disk's
00248D4C  var   vcStorageDevices  u32                   # the table of the storage devices' records
00248D50  var   vcStorageEvent  u32                     # what happened last to a storage device, until it is asked for (sub_0012DE80 hands it out and clears it): 0xB a memory unit put in, 0xC one taken out
00248D58  var   vcMemoryUnitsInserted  u32              # the memory units put in and not told of yet, a bit each
00248D5C  var   vcMemoryUnitsRemoved  u32               # the memory units taken out and not told of yet, a bit each

# The engine's vertex shaders on the device: the one set last, and where the next one loaded goes in the
# device's 136 slots of shader code (sub_001374A0 loads one at the next free address and starts over when
# it does not fit; sub_00137550 sets one if it is not the one set). Read by an agent and checked here.
00248D60  var   vcVertexShaderCurrent  u32              # the vertex shader set on the device last: setting the same again does nothing
00248D64  var   vcVertexShaderNextAddress  u32          # where the next vertex shader loaded goes in the device's shader code: past the last one's
00248D68  var   vcVertexShaderLoadedCount  u32          # the vertex shaders loaded since the device's shader code was started over

# The pad's items a mission has off: twelve words, 0 or 1, that DisableControllerItemsAction sets from the
# bits of its mask in this order (sub_000DEA10; a mask of 0 clears them all) and that the players' control
# code tests where it reads each control of vcPads; a level whose flags have 0x80 starts with the twelve on
# (sub_00011690). Read by an agent from Ghidra's C and checked here.
struct  ControllerItems  30                         # which of the pad's items are off, a word each
field   ControllerItems  00  x                        u32   # the X button
field   ControllerItems  04  b                        u32   # the B button
field   ControllerItems  08  y                        u32   # the Y button
field   ControllerItems  0C  white_black_right_thumb  u32   # the white and black buttons and the right stick pressed in
field   ControllerItems  10  right_stick              u32   # the right stick's directions
field   ControllerItems  14  dpad_left                u32   # the d-pad's left
field   ControllerItems  18  dpad_up_down             u32   # the d-pad's up and down
field   ControllerItems  1C  dpad_right               u32   # the d-pad's right
field   ControllerItems  20  triggers                 u32   # the two triggers
field   ControllerItems  24  a                        u32   # the A button
field   ControllerItems  28  left_x                   u32   # the left stick, across
field   ControllerItems  2C  left_y                   u32   # the left stick, up and down
002AEB20  var   ControllerItemsDisabled  ControllerItems

# A mech's motions: the record of 0x55 words an EntitySpec's motions points at for a type that walks. Its
# type's X_Startup clears it and fills it one motion at a time (vcObjectGet, class 9) and its X_Shutdown
# lets go of each. The shared code that moves an entity (sub_00050EF0, each frame) hands the record to
# sub_0004FB50, which picks one or two of its motions from the entity's speed along its three axes, its
# turning and its state, and blends them in the model's first layer (sub_001205C0); the names here are
# what that function plays each for, which is what the types load into each (the VF-1SX's "battloid bj
# fall back" at +0xB4). Read by an agent from Ghidra's C and checked here. Not named: +0x08 (a rate the
# walk-to-run blend plays at), +0x64 (the idle while the entity's flag 0x100 is on), +0xE0 (the walk of an
# entity whose +0x180 is 2: the veritech with the sumo pack) and +0xE4, which nothing reads.
struct  MechMotions  154                            # a walking type's motions and the sounds of its steps
field   MechMotions  000  walk_speed            u32   # points at the speed forward at which the walk plays whole: below it, idle and walk are blended
field   MechMotions  004  run_speed             u32   # points at the speed forward at which the run plays whole: between the two, walk and run are blended
field   MechMotions  00C  run_back_speed        u32   # points at the speed backward at which the run back plays whole
field   MechMotions  010  landing_sounds        u32[16]   # the sounds of a hard landing, played in turn
field   MechMotions  050  landing_sound_count   u32   # how many of them there are
field   MechMotions  054  landing_sound_next    u32   # which one plays next
field   MechMotions  058  slide_back            u32   # going backward faster than its top speed backward
field   MechMotions  05C  run_back              u32
field   MechMotions  060  idle                  u32
field   MechMotions  068  walk                  u32
field   MechMotions  06C  run                   u32
field   MechMotions  070  slide_forward         u32   # going forward faster than run_speed
field   MechMotions  074  slide_left            u32   # going left faster than its top speed sideways
field   MechMotions  078  strafe_left           u32
field   MechMotions  07C  strafe_right          u32
field   MechMotions  080  slide_right           u32
field   MechMotions  084  turn_left             u32   # turning in place
field   MechMotions  088  turn_right            u32
field   MechMotions  08C  rise                  u32   # going up in the air
field   MechMotions  090  land_soft             u32   # blended with land by how hard it comes down
field   MechMotions  094  land                  u32
field   MechMotions  098  thrust                u32   # in the air while it asks to go up: this, blended with the one of where it moves
field   MechMotions  09C  thrust_forward        u32
field   MechMotions  0A0  thrust_back           u32
field   MechMotions  0A4  thrust_left           u32
field   MechMotions  0A8  thrust_right          u32
field   MechMotions  0AC  fall                  u32   # falling: this, blended with the one of where it moves
field   MechMotions  0B0  fall_forward          u32
field   MechMotions  0B4  fall_back             u32
field   MechMotions  0B8  fall_left             u32
field   MechMotions  0BC  fall_right            u32
field   MechMotions  0C0  boost_back            u32   # while it boosts on the ground: the one of where it moves, alone
field   MechMotions  0C4  boost_forward         u32
field   MechMotions  0C8  boost_left            u32
field   MechMotions  0CC  boost_right           u32
field   MechMotions  0D0  alert                 u32   # the type's own code plays it, not the shared one
field   MechMotions  0D4  alarm                 u32   # the idle while the entity's flag 0x200 is on

# The cinematics: a movie is a Bink file the game plays by its name, to its end or until a button. The
# engine opens it from the folder vcMovie of the data, draws each frame into a texture and that on the whole
# screen (vcMovieOpen, vcMovieDrawFrame, vcMovieIsPlaying, vcMovieClose), and MoviePlay loops over them.
# Thirteen are played: TDKLogo, HarmonyLogo, VCLogo, Intro and RoboLegal when the game starts (sub_000124D0),
# Intro again from the main menu, and the story's, before a level or after the last (LevelPlayMovie,
# LevelPlayEndMovie): M100, M103, M104, M200, M300, M400, M500, M500Post. Bink's functions are named as its
# library calls them, told by what the engine's four do with each (BinkOpen is the one that answers "Not a
# Bink file.").
00016700  func  MovieSkipPressed
00016AF0  func  MoviePlay
00025360  func  LevelChapterNotStarted
000253C0  func  LevelPlayMovie
00025510  func  LevelPlayEndMovie
00118700  func  vcMovieSetSoundSystem
00118720  func  vcMovieIsPlaying
00118740  func  vcMovieOpen
00118940  func  vcMovieClose
00118980  func  vcMovieDrawFrame
00140730  func  vcMoviePath
00171C80  func  BinkSetSoundSystem
00172AA0  func  BinkDoFrame
00173100  func  BinkWait
00173660  func  BinkOpen
001745A0  func  BinkCopyToBuffer
001745E0  func  BinkNextFrame
00174780  func  BinkClose
00175370  func  BinkOpenDirectSound
param   MovieSkipPressed        arg0  any_button
param   MoviePlay               arg0  name
param   MoviePlay               arg1  can_skip
param   MoviePlay               arg2  any_button
param   LevelChapterNotStarted  arg0  chapter
param   LevelPlayMovie          arg0  level
param   LevelPlayEndMovie       arg0  level
comment MovieSkipPressed Whether a pad has just pressed A or START, or any of its buttons where any_button is set; takes that pad's presses away.
comment MoviePlay Plays the movie of a name on the whole screen, to its end, or until MovieSkipPressed where can_skip is set. Hands back 0 if there is no such movie.
comment LevelChapterNotStarted Whether no level of a chapter is finished yet.
comment LevelPlayMovie Plays the story's movie that goes before a level: a chapter's (M100, M200, M300, M400, M500) before its first levels while LevelChapterNotStarted, and M103 and M104 before levels 2 and 4.
comment LevelPlayEndMovie Plays the story's last movie (M500Post) once the last level is finished.
comment vcMovieSetSoundSystem Gives Bink the engine's DirectSound to play the movies' sound with (BinkSetSoundSystem, BinkOpenDirectSound).
comment vcMovieIsPlaying Whether a movie has frames left to show.
comment vcMovieOpen Opens the movie of a name (vcMoviePath and ".bik", BinkOpen) and makes the texture its frames are drawn into. Hands back 0 if Bink does not open it.
comment vcMovieClose Closes a movie (BinkClose) and lets go of its texture.
comment vcMovieDrawFrame Where it is time for it (BinkWait), has Bink work out the movie's next frame (BinkDoFrame), copies it into the texture (BinkCopyToBuffer), draws that on the whole screen, shows it and goes on to the frame after (BinkNextFrame). Hands back the number of the frame it is at then, -1 past the last; where it is not time yet, only that number.
comment vcMoviePath Writes where a movie is: the data's folder, the platform's, vcMovie and the name.
